This one, after she left, it just... it's hit me the hardest.\n\nDoctor: Thinking back over the past year, did those earlier low periods last at least two weeks each time, and did you feel back to your normal self in between them?\n\nPatient: I couldn't really tell you. I just don't care about it right now.\n\nDoctor: Over these past two weeks, have you noticed any changes in your sleep, like trouble falling asleep, waking up during the night, or sleeping much more or less than usual?\n\nPatient: I don't know... I'm not really keeping track of it.\n\nDoctor: Between those earlier low periods this past year, can you recall ever going two months or longer where you felt essentially back to your normal self, without any low mood at all?\n\nPatient: I don't really know. I don't keep a calendar of how I feel. Sometimes I'm fine, sometimes I'm not. I don't think I've ever counted the months between.\n\nDoctor: During those milder low periods over the past year, did you also notice things like changes in your appetite or weight, or feelings of guilt or worthlessness, along with feeling down?\n\nPatient: I eat fine, I guess. Haven't noticed my clothes fitting any different. And... I don't know about that other stuff. Guilt, or whatever. I don't really sit around dwelling on things like that. I just... I don't remember it being an issue back then.\n\nDoctor: During those earlier low periods this past year, did they ever interfere with your ability to function at work or with your family, or were you always able to carry on as usual despite feeling low?\n\nPatient: I just... I don't think it changed much.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
